Effect-size direction: the matched-pairs rank-biserial r is positive when algorithm a has larger gaps than algorithm b for the pair (a, b) as listed.
Declared in advance: with 6 instances the pairwise cross-instance Wilcoxon signed-rank test after Holm cannot reach significance at alpha (the smallest possible adjusted p-value is above alpha), so pairwise evidence comes from the per-instance Mann-Whitney U tests over the replicates and the cross-instance evidence from the Friedman test with Kendall's W; there is no correction across instances, and no instance is added after results are seen.
With 6 matched units the smallest possible exact two-sided Wilcoxon p is 0.03125; after Holm over 45 pairs the smallest possible adjusted p is 1, above alpha 0.05: the pairwise cross-instance tests cannot reach significance here.
Conventions (from the analysis plan): Mann-Whitney method auto, continuity correction True; Wilcoxon method auto, continuity correction False, zero differences pratt; significance p <= alpha; Holm within comparison cell, no correction across instances.
Statistical results describe these runs only; the report states no ordering of the algorithms.

Effect-size direction: the matched-pairs rank-biserial r is positive when algorithm a has larger gaps than algorithm b for the pair (a, b) as listed.
Declared in advance: with 6 instances the pairwise cross-instance Wilcoxon signed-rank test after Holm cannot reach significance at alpha (the smallest possible adjusted p-value is above alpha), so pairwise evidence comes from the per-instance Mann-Whitney U tests over the replicates and the cross-instance evidence from the Friedman test with Kendall's W; there is no correction across instances, and no instance is added after results are seen.
With 6 matched units the smallest possible exact two-sided Wilcoxon p is 0.03125; after Holm over 45 pairs the smallest possible adjusted p is 1, above alpha 0.05: the pairwise cross-instance tests cannot reach significance here.
Conventions (from the analysis plan): Mann-Whitney method auto, continuity correction True; Wilcoxon method auto, continuity correction False, zero differences pratt; significance p <= alpha; Holm within comparison cell, no correction across instances.
Statistical results describe these runs only; the report states no ordering of the algorithms.

Effect-size direction: the matched-pairs rank-biserial r is positive when algorithm a has larger gaps than algorithm b for the pair (a, b) as listed.
Declared in advance: with 6 instances the pairwise cross-instance Wilcoxon signed-rank test after Holm cannot reach significance at alpha (the smallest possible adjusted p-value is above alpha), so pairwise evidence comes from the per-instance Mann-Whitney U tests over the replicates and the cross-instance evidence from the Friedman test with Kendall's W; there is no correction across instances, and no instance is added after results are seen.
With 6 matched units the smallest possible exact two-sided Wilcoxon p is 0.03125; after Holm over 45 pairs the smallest possible adjusted p is 1, above alpha 0.05: the pairwise cross-instance tests cannot reach significance here.
Conventions (from the analysis plan): Mann-Whitney method auto, continuity correction True; Wilcoxon method auto, continuity correction False, zero differences pratt; significance p <= alpha; Holm within comparison cell, no correction across instances.
Statistical results describe these runs only; the report states no ordering of the algorithms.
